{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Base directory for this skill: /ssd/workspace/buidlguidl/ethskills-evals-test/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-001/2026-08-19T184304Z-claude-with-skill-2/workspace/.claude/skills/frontend-playbook\n\n# Frontend Playbook\n\nFollow this workflow; do not treat a green build or successful upload as proof that the intended app shipped.\n\n## Start with the requested stack\n\nWhen the user wants Scaffold-ETH 2 or has not selected a stack for a conventional contract-plus-frontend dApp, use its generator:\n\n```bash\nnpx create-eth@2.0.23 # Tested version; update deliberately\n```\n\nUse the generated Foundry/Next.js monorepo, wallet integration, contract hooks, and components. Respect an explicitly requested alternative stack.\n\n## Choose the local chain deliberately\n\n- Use `yarn chain` for isolated contracts, mocks, and unit tests.\n- Use `yarn fork --network <chain>` when behavior depends on deployed protocols, tokens, balances, or other real chain state.\n- In fork mode, point the frontend at the local Anvil network (`chains.foundry`, chain ID 31337), not the upstream chain being copied. Switch to the real target chain only for a real deployment.\n\nA fork also grants powers the real chain does not. Fund demo identities from state that already exists rather than deploying a mock token:\n\n```bash\ncast rpc anvil_impersonateAccount <whale>\ncast send <token> \"transfer(address,uint256)\" <demo-account> <amount> \\\n  --from <whale> --unlocked\n```\n\n`anvil_setBalance` and `anvil_setStorageAt` are equivalent overrides when no suitable holder exists. Nothing here is broadcast; the fork is a local copy.\n\nAnvil normally mines only when a transaction arrives, so between transactions the latest block and `block.timestamp` remain frozen; the next transaction advances time in one jump. This silently breaks live deadlines, expiry, and vesting displays even when `vm.warp` unit tests pass. For continuous behavior, enable interval mining:\n\n```bash\ncast rpc anvil_setIntervalMining 1\n```\n\nFor a frozen live demo, enable interval mining. Mining a single block with `evm_mine` restamps `block.timestamp` once and it freezes again immediately; manual mining and `evm_increaseTime` are for controlled single-step tests, not a running demo. Add `--block-time 1` to the fork script when the project should always run that way.\n\n## Build a static IPFS release\n\nConfigure the IPFS build for static export, route directories, and static images:\n\n```typescript\nif (process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_IPFS_BUILD === \"true\") {\n  nextConfig.output = \"export\";\n  nextConfig.trailingSlash = true;\n  nextConfig.images = { unoptimized: true };\n}\n```\n\nSet the production origin before building so Open Graph URLs and images do not resolve to localhost. Then remove old artifacts and rebuild:\n\n```bash\ncd packages/nextjs\nrm -rf .next out\nNEXT_PUBLIC_PRODUCTION_URL=\"https://<production-domain>\" \\\n  NEXT_PUBLIC_IPFS_BUILD=true \\\n  yarn build\n```\n\nOn Node 25, the built-in `localStorage` global can exist without the standard Web Storage methods when no backing file is configured; libraries detect it and crash on calls such as `getItem()` during static prerender. Apply a process-level remedy inherited by build workers, not only code in `instrumentation.ts` or `next.config.ts`. Depending on the app, use one of:\n\n```bash\nNODE_OPTIONS=\"--localstorage-file=.node-localstorage\"\nNODE_OPTIONS=\"--no-experimental-webstorage\"\n```\n\n## Verify before and after upload\n\nBefore uploading, confirm the expected change is in `out/`, that every route emitted its own directory, and that generated metadata carries the production origin:\n\n```bash\nls out/*/index.html # One directory with an index.html per route\n```\n\nAfter uploading:\n\n1. Record the CID. An unchanged CID means the uploaded bytes are identical; check the build output and upload target before blaming gateway caching.\n2. Load the root and at least one non-home route through the gateway. Root success does not prove exported routes resolve.\n\n   ```bash\n   curl -s -o /dev/null -w \"%{http_code}\" -L \"https://<gateway>/ipfs/<cid>/debug/\" # Expect 200, not 404\n   ```\n\n3. Verify the Open Graph image and URL use the production origin.\n4. For ENS, update the content hash only after the deployed CID has been reviewed and approved, then confirm the resolver and public gateway serve that CID.\n\nDo not claim the release is complete until these checks pass.\n"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"629310d9-136f-4f61-a7ad-95f98c72edf2","uuid":"20140b29-8c83-4a91-b367-47bd8dfae083","timestamp":"2026-08-19T18:43:10.597Z","isSynthetic":true}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01FchegTn8B9f1Kge3MZqUvn","type":"tool_result","content":"===FOUNDRY===\npackages/foundry/contracts:\nYourContract.sol\n\npackages/foundry/script:\nDeployHelpers.s.sol\nDeploy.s.sol\nDeployYourContract.s.sol\nVerifyAll.s.sol\n\npackages/foundry/test:\nYourContract.t.sol\n===NEXTJS APP===\nblockexplorer\ndebug\nlayout.tsx\nnot-found.tsx\npage.tsx\n===SCAFFOLD CONFIG===\nimport * as chains from \"viem/chains\";\n\n\n\nexport type BaseConfig = {\n  targetNetworks: readonly chains.Chain[];\n  pollingInterval: number;\n  alchemyApiKey: string;\n  rpcOverrides?: Record<number, string>;\n  walletConnectProjectId: string;\n  burnerWalletMode: \"localNetworksOnly\" | \"allNetworks\" | \"disabled\";\n};\n\nexport type ScaffoldConfig = BaseConfig ;\n\nexport const DEFAULT_ALCHEMY_API_KEY = \"IZYEU2cWBgnFmgiTAgpWD\";\n\nconst scaffoldConfig = {\n  // The networks on which your DApp is live\n  targetNetworks: [\n    chains.foundry\n  ],\n  // The interval at which your front-end polls the RPC servers for new data (it has no effect if you only target the local an env variable:\n  // .env.local for local testing, and in the Vercel/system env config for live apps.\n  alchemyApiKey: process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_ALCHEMY_API_KEY || DEFAULT_ALCHEMY_API_KEY,\n  // If {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01JedHKrgnGVv7pa9EzoKntA","type":"tool_result","content":"===YourContract.sol===\n//SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity >=0.8.0 <0.9.0;\n\n// Useful for debugging. Remove when deploying to a live network.\nimport \"forge-std/console.sol\";\n\n// Use openzeppelin to inherit battle-tested implementations (ERC20, ERC721, etc)\n// import \"@openzeppelin/contracts/access/Ownable.sol\";\n\n/**\n * A smart contract that allows changing a state variable of the contract and tracking the changes\n * It also allows the owner to withdraw the Ether in the contract\n * @author BuidlGuidl\n */\ncontract YourContract {\n  // State Variables\n  address public immutable owner;\n  string public greeting = \"Building Unstoppable Apps!!!\";\n  bool public premium = false;\n  uint256 public totalCounter = 0;\n  mapping(address => uint256) public userGreetingCounter;\n\n  // Events: a way to emit log statements from smart contract that can be listened to by external parties\n  event GreetingChange(\n    address indexed greetingSetter,\n    string newGreeting,\n    bool premium,\n    uint256 value\n  );\n\n  // Constructor: Called once on contract deployment\n  // Check packages/foundry/deploy/Deploy.s.sol\n  constructor(address _owner) {\n    owner = _owner;\n  }\n\n  // Modifier: used to define a set of rules that must be met before or after a function is executed\n  // Check the withdraw() function\n  modifier isOwner() {\n    // msg.sender: predefined variable that represents address of the account that called the current function\n    require(msg.sender == owner, \"Not the Owner\");\n    _;\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Function that allows anyone to change the state variable \"greeting\" of the contract and increase the counters\n   *\n   * @param _newGreeting (string memory) - new greeting to save on the contract\n   */\n  function setGreeting(string memory _newGreeting) public payable {\n    // Print data to the anvil chain console. Remove when deploying to a live network.\n\n    console.logString(\"Setting new greeting\");\n    console.logString(_newGreeting);\n\n    greeting = _newGreeting;\n    totalCounter += 1;\n    userGreetingCounter[msg.sender] += 1;\n\n    // msg.value: built-in global variable that represents the amount of ether sent with the transaction\n    if (msg.value > 0) {\n      premium = true;\n    } else {\n      premium = false;\n    }\n\n    // emit: keyword used to trigger an event\n    emit GreetingChange(msg.sender, _newGreeting, msg.value > 0, msg.value);\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Function that allows the owner to withdraw all the Ether in the contract\n   * The function can only be called by the owner of the contract as defined by the isOwner modifier\n   */\n  function withdraw() public isOwner {\n    (bool success,) = owner.call{ value: address(this).balance }(\"\");\n    require(success, \"Failed to send Ether\");\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Function that allows the contract to receive ETH\n   */\n  receive() external payable { }\n}\n===DeployYourContract===\n// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity ^0.8.19;\n\nimport \"./DeployHelpers.s.sol\";\nimport \"../contracts/YourContract.sol\";\n\n/**\n * @notice Deploy script for YourContract contract\n * @dev Inherits ScaffoldETHDeploy which:\n *      - Includes forge-std/Script.sol for deployment\n *      - Includes ScaffoldEthDeployerRunner modifier\n *      - Provides `deployer` variable\n * Example:\n * yarn deploy --file DeployYourContract.s.sol  # local anvil chain\n * yarn deploy --file DeployYourContract.s.sol --network optimism # live network (requires keystore)\n */\ncontract DeployYourContract is ScaffoldETHDeploy {\n    /**\n     * @dev Deployer setup based on `ETH_KEYSTORE_ACCOUNT` in `.env`:\n     *      - \"scaffold-eth-default\": Uses Anvil's account #9 (0xa0Ee7A142d267C1f36714E4a8F75612F20a79720), no password prompt\n     *      - \"scaffold-eth-custom\": requires password used while creating keystore\n     *\n     * Note: Must use ScaffoldEthDeployerRunner modifier to:\n     *      - Setup correct `deployer` account and fund it\n     *      - Export contract addresses & ABIs to `nextjs` packages\n     */\n    function run() external ScaffoldEthDeployerRunner {\n        new YourContract(deployer);\n    }\n}\n===Deploy.s.sol===\n//SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity ^0.8.19;\n\nimport \"./DeployHelpers.s.sol\";\nimport { DeployYourContract } from \"./DeployYourContract.s.sol\";\n\n/**\n * @notice Main deployment script for all contracts\n * @dev Run this when you want to deploy multiple contracts at once\n *\n * Example: yarn deploy # runs this script(without`--file` flag)\n */\ncontract DeployScript is ScaffoldETHDeploy {\n  function run() external {\n    // Deploys all your contracts sequentially\n    // Add new deployments here when needed\n\n    \n    DeployYourContract deployYourContract = new DeployYourContract();\n    deployYourContract.run();\n\n\n    // Deploy another contract\n    // DeployMyContract myContract = new DeployMyContract();\n    // myContract.run();\n  }\n}","is_error":false}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"629310d9-136f-4f61-a7ad-95f98c72edf2","uuid":"33eed3f7-76f8-45b3-8392-ecd15b8a4147","timestamp":"2026-08-19T18:43:27.295Z","tool_use_result":{"stdout":"===YourContract.sol===\n//SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity >=0.8.0 <0.9.0;\n\n// Useful for debugging. {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_014UjFiU5fkSs6nJXbkQfmEE","type":"tool_result","content":"chain: setup-anvil-wallet\n\tanvil\n\n# Start a fork\nfork: setup-anvil-wallet\n\tanvil --fork-url ${FORK_URL} --chain-id 31337\n\n# Deploy the contracts\ndeploy:\n\t@if [ ! -f \"$(DEPLOY_SCRIPT)\" ]; then \t\techo \"Error: Deploy script '$(DEPLOY_SCRIPT)' not found\"; \t\texit 1; \tfi\n\t@if [ \"$(RPC_URL)\" = \"localhost\" ]; then \t\tif [ \"$(ETH_KEYSTORE_ACCOUNT)\" = \"scaffold-eth-default\" ]; then \t\t\tforge script $(DEPLOY_SCRIPT) --rpc-url localhost --password localhost --broadcast --ffi; \t\telse \t\t\tforge script $(DEPLOY_SCRIPT) --rpc-url localhost --broadcast --ffi; \t\tfi \telse \t\tforge script $(DEPLOY_SCRIPT) --rpc-url $(RPC_URL) --broadcast --ffi; \tfi\n\n# Deploy and generate ABIs\ndeploy-and-generate-abis: deploy generate-abis \n\n===ENV EXAMPLE===\n# Template for foundry environment variables.\n\n# For local development, copy this file, rename it to .env, and fill in the values.\n\n# We provide default values so developers can start prototyping out of the box,\n# but we recommend getting your own API Keys for Production Apps.\n\n# DEPLOYER_PRIVATE_KEY is used while deploying contract.\n# On anvil chain the value of it can be empty since we use the prefunded account\n# which comes with anvil chain to deploy contract.\n# NOTE: You don't need to manually change the value of DEPLOYER_PRIVATE_KEY, it should\n# be auto filled when run `yarn generate`.\n\n# Alchemy rpc URL is used while deploying the contracts to some testnets/mainnets, checkout `foundry.toml` for it's use.\nALCHEMY_API_KEY=IZYEU2cWBgnFmgiTAgpWD\n# Etherscan API key is used to verify the contract on etherscan.\nETHERSCAN_API_KEY=DNXJA8RX2Q3VZ4URQIWP7Z68CJXQZSC6AW\n# Default account for localhost / use \"scaffold-eth-custom\" if you wish to use a generated account or imported account\nLOCALHOST_KEYSTORE_ACCOUNT=scaffold-eth-default\n===scaffoldConfig scaffold-eth setup-anvil-wallet\n\tanvil\n\n# Start a fork\nfork: setup-anvil-wallet\n\tanvil --fork-url ${FORK_URL} --chain-id 31337\n\n# Deploy the contracts\ndeploy:\n\t@if [ ! -f \"$(DEPLOY_SCRIPT)\" ]; then \t\techo \"Error: Deploy script '$(DEPLOY_SCRIPT)' not found\"; \t\texit 1; \tfi\n\t@if [ \"$(RPC_URL)\" = \"localhost\" ]; then \t\tif [ \"$(ETH_KEYSTORE_ACCOUNT)\" = \"scaffold-eth-default\" ]; then \t\t\tforge script $(DEPLOY_SCRIPT) --rpc-url localhost --password localhost --broadcast --ffi; \t\telse \t\t\tforge script $(DEPLOY_SCRIPT) --rpc-url localhost --broadcast --ffi; \t\tfi \telse \t\tforge script $(DEPLOY_SCRIPT) --rpc-url $(RPC_URL) --broadcast --ffi; \tfi\n\n# Deploy and generate ABIs\ndeploy-and-generate-abis: deploy generate-abis \n\n===ENV EXAMPLE===\n# Template for foundry environment variables.\n\n# For local development, copy this file, rename it to .env, and fill in the values.\n\n# We provide default values so developers can start prototyping out of the box,\n# but we recommend getting your own API Keys for Production Apps.\n\n# DEPLOYER_PRIVATE_KEY is used while deploying contract.\n# On anvil chain the value of it can be empty since we use the prefunded account\n# which comes with anvil chain to deploy contract.\n# NOTE: You don't need to manually change the value of DEPLOYER_PRIVATE_KEY, it should\n# be auto filled when run `yarn generate`.\n\n# Alchemy rpc URL is used while deploying the contracts to some testnets/mainnets, checkout `foundry.toml` for it's use.\nALCHEMY_API_KEY=IZYEU2cWBgnFmgiTAgpWD\n# Etherscan API key is used to verify the contract on etherscan.\nETHERSCAN_API_KEY=DNXJA8RX2Q3VZ4URQIWP7Z68CJXQZSC6AW\n# Default account for localhost / use \"scaffold-eth-custom\" if you wish to use a generated account or imported account\nLOCALHOST_KEYSTORE_ACCOUNT=scaffold-eth-default\n===scaffoldConfig scaffold-eth {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CeCboj1Y9eaqezec8oamV","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Now I understand the project. Let me build the TipJar contract first."}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1011,"cache_read_input_tokens":31727,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1011},"output_tokens":5,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"629310d9-136f-4f61-a7ad-95f98c72edf2","uuid":"eebf0972-4070-4c97-b2d7-e172e6aba2df","timestamp":"2026-08-19T18:44:53.677Z","request_id":"req_011CeCbogsoDxY44EuzrPKGW"}
{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CeCboj1Y9eaqezec8oamV","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01MzxgKSN8BDEmECVaUjFA52","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/ssd/workspace/buidlguidl/ethskills-evals-test/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-001/2026-08-19T184304Z-claude-with-skill-2/workspace/packages/foundry/contracts/TipJar.sol","content":"//SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity >=0.8.0 <0.9.0;\n\nimport { IERC20 } from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/IERC20.sol\";\nimport { SafeERC20 } from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/utils/SafeERC20.sol\";\nimport { Ownable } from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/access/Ownable.sol\";\n\n/**\n * @notice A tip jar that collects USDC tips and keeps an on-chain message feed.\n * @dev Tips are pulled with `transferFrom`, so a tipper must `approve` the jar\n *      before calling `tip`. The token is fixed at deploy time so the same\n *      contract runs against Base's real USDC on a fork, on a testnet, or on\n *      mainnet. USDC uses 6 decimals: 1 USDC == 1_000_000.\n * @author BuidlGuidl\n */\ncontract TipJar is Ownable {\n    using SafeERC20 for IERC20;\n\n    struct Tip {\n        address tipper;\n        uint256 amount;\n        uint256 timestamp;\n        string message;\n    }\n\n    IERC20 public immutable usdc;\n    Tip[] public tips;\n    mapping(address => uint256) public totalTippedBy;\n    uint256 public totalTipped;\n\n    event NewTip(uint256 indexed index, address indexed tipper, uint256 amount, string message);\n    event Withdrawn(address indexed to, uint256 amount);\n\n    error ZeroAmount();\n\n    constructor(address _owner, IERC20 _usdc) Ownable(_owner) {\n        usdc = _usdc;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Send a USDC tip with an optional message. Requires a prior\n     *         `approve(tipJar, amount)` on the USDC contract.\n     * @param amount USDC amount in 6-decimal units (1 USDC == 1_000_000).\n     * @param message Public message stored alongside the tip.\n     */\n    function tip(uint256 amount, string calldata message) external {\n        if (amount == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n        usdc.safeTransferFrom(msg.sender, address(this), amount);\n\n        tips.push(Tip({ tipper: msg.sender, amount: amount, timestamp: block.timestamp, message: message }));\n        totalTippedBy[msg.sender] += amount;\n        totalTipped += amount;\n\n        emit NewTip(tips.length - 1, msg.sender, amount, message);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Total number of tips ever recorded.\n    function tipCount() external view returns (uint256) {\n        return tips.length;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Returns up to `limit` most recent tips, newest first. This powers\n     *         the feed without an event-log scan, which is unreliable on a fork\n     *         where the starting block number is far from genesis.\n     */\n    function recentTips(uint256 limit) external view returns (Tip[] memory) {\n        uint256 count = tips.length;\n        if (limit > count) {\n            limit = count;\n        }\n        Tip[] memory result = new Tip[](limit);\n        for (uint256 i = 0; i < limit; i++) {\n            result[i] = tips[count - 1 - i];\n        }\n        return result;\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Owner sweeps the full USDC balance out of the jar.\n    function withdraw() external onlyOwner {\n        uint256 balance = usdc.balanceOf(address(this));\n        usdc.safeTransfer(owner(), balance);\n        emit Withdrawn(owner(), balance);\n    }\n}\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1011,"cache_read_input_tokens":31727,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1011},"output_tokens":5,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"629310d9-136f-4f61-a7ad-95f98c72edf2","uuid":"d1a3bcac-d339-4633-ab69-6aae3c45e19a","timestamp":"2026-08-19T18:45:06.992Z","request_id":"req_011CeCbogsoDxY44EuzrPKGW"}

The token is fixed at deploy time so the same\n *      contract runs against Base's real USDC on a fork, on a testnet, or on\n *      mainnet. USDC uses 6 decimals: 1 USDC == 1_000_000.\n * @author BuidlGuidl\n */\ncontract TipJar is Ownable {\n    using SafeERC20 for IERC20;\n\n    struct Tip {\n        address tipper;\n        uint256 amount;\n        uint256 timestamp;\n        string message;\n    }\n\n    IERC20 public immutable usdc;\n    Tip[] public tips;\n    mapping(address => uint256) public totalTippedBy;\n    uint256 public totalTipped;\n\n    event NewTip(uint256 indexed index, address indexed tipper, uint256 amount, string message);\n    event Withdrawn(address indexed to, uint256 amount);\n\n    error ZeroAmount();\n\n    constructor(address _owner, IERC20 _usdc) Ownable(_owner) {\n        usdc = _usdc;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Send a USDC tip with an optional message. This powers\n     *         the feed without an event-log scan, which is unreliable on a fork\n     *         where the starting block number is far from genesis.\n     */\n    function recentTips(uint256 limit) external view returns (Tip[] memory) {\n        uint256 count = tips.length;\n        if (limit > count) {\n            limit = count;\n        }\n        Tip[] memory result = new Tip[](limit);\n        for (uint256 i = 0; i < limit; i++) {\n            result[i] = tips[count - 1 - i];\n        }\n        return result;\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Owner sweeps the full USDC balance out of the jar.\n    function withdraw() external onlyOwner {\n        uint256 balance = usdc.balanceOf(address(this));\n        usdc.safeTransfer(owner(), balance);\n        emit Withdrawn(owner(), balance);\n    }\n}\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CeCbvkE9s6tYxkf1aWjVG","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_017h5vPoRV2Dwvu3ePAMdA8v","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/ssd/workspace/buidlguidl/ethskills-evals-test/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-001/2026-08-19T184304Z-claude-with-skill-2/workspace/packages/foundry/test/TipJar.t.sol","content":"// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity ^0.8.19;\n\nimport { Test } from \"forge-std/Test.sol\";\nimport { ERC20 } from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/ERC20.sol\";\nimport { IERC20 } from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/IERC20.sol\";\nimport { Ownable } from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/access/Ownable.sol\";\nimport { TipJar } from \"../contracts/TipJar.sol\";\n\n/// @dev Minimal 6-decimal stand-in for USDC used by the unit tests. The real\n///      USDC is exercised separately via `yarn fork --network base`.\ncontract MockUSDC is ERC20 {\n    constructor() ERC20(\"USD Coin\", \"USDC\") { }\n\n    function decimals() public pure override returns (uint8) {\n        return 6;\n    }\n\n    function mint(address to, uint256 amount) external {\n        _mint(to, amount);\n    }\n}\n\ncontract TipJarTest is Test {\n    MockUSDC usdc;\n    TipJar jar;\n\n    address owner = makeAddr(\"owner\");\n    address alice = makeAddr(\"alice\");\n    address bob = makeAddr(\"bob\");\n\n    uint256 constant ONE_USDC = 1e6;\n\n    function setUp() public {\n        usdc = new MockUSDC();\n        jar = new TipJar(owner, IERC20(address(usdc)));\n        usdc.mint(alice, 100 * ONE_USDC);\n        usdc.mint(bob, 100 * ONE_USDC);\n    }\n\n    function _tip(address who, uint256 amount, string memory message) internal {\n        vm.startPrank(who);\n        usdc.approve(address(jar), amount);\n        jar.tip(amount, message);\n        vm.stopPrank();\n    }\n\n    function test_TipPullsUsdcAndRecordsFeed() public {\n        _tip(alice, 5 * ONE_USDC, \"great work\");\n\n        assertEq(usdc.balanceOf(address(jar)), 5 * ONE_USDC);\n        assertEq(usdc.balanceOf(alice), 95 * ONE_USDC);\n        assertEq(jar.tipCount(), 1);\n        assertEq(jar.totalTipped(), 5 * ONE_USDC);\n        assertEq(jar.totalTippedBy(alice), 5 * ONE_USDC);\n\n        (address tipper, uint256 amount,, string memory message) = jar.tips(0);\n        assertEq(tipper, alice);\n        assertEq(amount, 5 * ONE_USDC);\n        assertEq(message, \"great work\");\n    }\n\n    function test_RecentTipsAreNewestFirstAndCapped() public {\n        _tip(alice, 1 * ONE_USDC, \"first\");\n        _tip(bob, 2 * ONE_USDC, \"second\");\n        _tip(alice, 3 * ONE_USDC, \"third\");\n\n        TipJar.Tip[] memory recent = jar.recentTips(2);\n        assertEq(recent.length, 2);\n        assertEq(recent[0].message, \"third\");\n        assertEq(recent[1].message, \"second\");\n\n        // limit larger than count returns everything without reverting.\n        assertEq(jar.recentTips(50).length, 3);\n    }\n\n    function test_TipRevertsOnZeroAmount() public {\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        vm.expectRevert(TipJar.ZeroAmount.selector);\n        jar.tip(0, \"nope\");\n    }\n\n    function test_TipRevertsWithoutApproval() public {\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        vm.expectRevert();\n        jar.tip(ONE_USDC, \"no approval\");\n    }\n\n    function test_OnlyOwnerWithdraws() public {\n        _tip(alice, 10 * ONE_USDC, \"thanks\");\n\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        vm.expectRevert(abi.encodeWithSelector(Ownable.OwnableUnauthorizedAccount.selector, alice));\n        jar.withdraw();\n\n        vm.prank(owner);\n        jar.withdraw();\n        assertEq(usdc.balanceOf(owner), 10 * ONE_USDC);\n        assertEq(usdc.balanceOf(address(jar)), 0);\n    }\n}\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":216,"cache_read_input_tokens":40251,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":216},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"629310d9-136f-4f61-a7ad-95f98c72edf2","uuid":"1a860bbe-5df2-4eeb-9750-69fc322008a2","timestamp":"2026-08-19T18:45:48.638Z","request_id":"req_011CeCbvjMa2f9xJeq5Ya2zn"}

message);\n        vm.stopPrank();\n    }\n\n    function test_TipPullsUsdcAndRecordsFeed() public {\n        _tip(alice, 5 * ONE_USDC, \"great work\");\n\n        assertEq(usdc.balanceOf(address(jar)), 5 * ONE_USDC);\n        assertEq(usdc.balanceOf(alice), 95 * ONE_USDC);\n        assertEq(jar.tipCount(), 1);\n        assertEq(jar.totalTipped(), 5 * ONE_USDC);\n        assertEq(jar.totalTippedBy(alice), 5 * ONE_USDC);\n\n        (address tipper, uint256 amount,, string memory message) = jar.tips(0);\n        assertEq(tipper, alice);\n        assertEq(amount, 5 * ONE_USDC);\n        assertEq(message, \"great work\");\n    }\n\n    function test_RecentTipsAreNewestFirstAndCapped() public {\n        _tip(alice, 1 * ONE_USDC, \"first\");\n        _tip(bob, 2 * ONE_USDC, \"second\");\n        _tip(alice, 3 * ONE_USDC, \"third\");\n\n        TipJar.Tip[] memory recent = jar.recentTips(2);\n        assertEq(recent.length, 2);\n assertEq(jar.recentTips(50).length, 3);\n    }\n\n    function test_TipRevertsOnZeroAmount() public {\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        vm.expectRevert(TipJar.ZeroAmount.selector);\n        jar.tip(0, \"nope\");\n    }\n\n    function test_TipRevertsWithoutApproval() public {\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        vm.expectRevert();\n        jar.tip(ONE_USDC, \"no approval\");\n    }\n\n    function test_OnlyOwnerWithdraws() public {\n        _tip(alice, 10 * ONE_USDC, \"thanks\");\n\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        vm.expectRevert(abi.encodeWithSelector(Ownable.OwnableUnauthorizedAccount.selector, alice));\n        jar.withdraw();\n\n        vm.prank(owner);\n        jar.withdraw();\n {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_016cEQ4fiGDzkKjNYg8u4wtf","type":"tool_result","content":".PHONY: build deploy generate-abis get-address account chain compile flatten fork format lint test verify\n\nDEPLOY_SCRIPT ?= script/Deploy.s.sol\n\n# setup wallet for anvil\nsetup-anvil-wallet:\n\tshx rm ~/.foundry/keystores/scaffold-eth-default 2>/dev/null; \tshx rm -rf broadcast/Deploy.s.sol/31337\n\tcast wallet import --private-key 0x2a871d0798f97d79848a013d4936a73bf4cc922c825d33c1cf7073dff6d409c6 --unsafe-password 'localhost' scaffold-eth-default\n\n# Start local chain\nchain: setup-anvil-wallet\n\tanvil\n\n# Start a fork\nfork: setup-anvil-wallet\n\tanvil --fork-url ${FORK_URL} --chain-id 31337\n\n# Deploy the contracts\ndeploy:\n\t@if [ ! -f \"$(DEPLOY_SCRIPT)\" ]; then \t\t\tforge script $(DEPLOY_SCRIPT) --rpc-url localhost --password localhost --broadcast --ffi; \t\telse \t\t\tforge script $(DEPLOY_SCRIPT) --rpc-url localhost --broadcast --ffi; \t\tfi \telse \t\tforge script $(DEPLOY_SCRIPT) --rpc-url $(RPC_URL) --broadcast --ffi; {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CeCcPfQwJCVAT8feakqmm","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_012vEzJwYF3qaw1NZJhDjLqk","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/ssd/workspace/buidlguidl/ethskills-evals-test/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-001/2026-08-19T184304Z-claude-with-skill-2/workspace/packages/nextjs/components/tipjar/TipFeed.tsx","content":"\"use client\";\n\nimport { formatUnits } from \"viem\";\nimport { Address } from \"@scaffold-ui/components\";\nimport { useScaffoldReadContract } from \"~~/hooks/scaffold-eth\";\n\nconst USDC_DECIMALS = 6;\n\nconst formatUsdc = (amount: bigint) =>\n  Number(formatUnits(amount, USDC_DECIMALS)).toLocaleString(undefined, {\n    minimumFractionDigits: 2,\n    maximumFractionDigits: 2,\n  });\n\nconst formatWhen = (timestamp: bigint) => new Date(Number(timestamp) * 1000).toLocaleString();\n\nexport const TipFeed = () => {\n  const { data: tips, isLoading } = useScaffoldReadContract({\n    contractName: \"TipJar\",\n    functionName: \"recentTips\",\n    args: [20n],\n    watch: true,\n  });\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"card bg-base-100 shadow-xl\">\n      <div className=\"card-body\">\n        <h2 className=\"card-title\">Recent tips</h2>\n\n        {isLoading && <span className=\"loading loading-spinner loading-md\" />}\n\n        {!isLoading && (!tips || tips.length === 0) && (\n          <p className=\"text-base-content/70\">No tips yet. Be the first to drop some USDC.</p>\n        )}\n\n        <ul className=\"flex flex-col gap-3\">\n          {tips?.map((tip, index) => (\n            <li key={index} className=\"flex flex-col gap-1 border-b border-base-300 pb-3 last:border-0\">\n              <div className=\"flex items-center justify-between gap-2\">\n                <Address address={tip.tipper} size=\"sm\" />\n                <span className=\"badge badge-primary whitespace-nowrap\">{formatUsdc(tip.amount)} USDC</span>\n              </div>\n              {tip.message && <p className=\"break-words\">{tip.message}</p>}\n              <span className=\"text-xs text-base-content/60\">{formatWhen(tip.timestamp)}</span>\n            </li>\n          ))}\n        </ul>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":493,"cache_read_input_tokens":65444,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":493},"output_tokens":5,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"629310d9-136f-4f61-a7ad-95f98c72edf2","uuid":"41ecf744-709e-4771-bb2d-5b95f92d9d7f","timestamp":"2026-08-19T18:51:46.963Z","request_id":"req_011CeCcPeNBWmwFhD5qo89kx"}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CeCcQVi4dsNZGMkbXEZZt","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01F7GheWLc8THPhXFUtFgpvF","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/ssd/workspace/buidlguidl/ethskills-evals-test/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-001/2026-08-19T184304Z-claude-with-skill-2/workspace/packages/nextjs/components/tipjar/TipForm.tsx","content":"\"use client\";\n\nimport { useState } from \"react\";\nimport { formatUnits, parseUnits } from \"viem\";\nimport { useAccount } from \"wagmi\";\nimport { useDeployedContractInfo, useScaffoldReadContract, useScaffoldWriteContract } from \"~~/hooks/scaffold-eth\";\nimport { notification } from \"~~/utils/scaffold-eth\";\n\nconst USDC_DECIMALS = 6;\n\nexport const TipForm = () => {\n  const { address: connectedAddress } = useAccount();\n  const [amount, setAmount] = useState(\"\");\n  const [message, setMessage] = useState(\"\");\n\n  const { data: tipJar } = useDeployedContractInfo({ contractName: \"TipJar\" });\n  const { data: balance } = useScaffoldReadContract({\n    contractName: \"USDC\",\n    functionName: \"balanceOf\",\n    args: [connectedAddress],\n    watch: true,\n  });\n  const { data: allowance } = useScaffoldReadContract({\n    contractName: \"USDC\",\n    functionName: \"allowance\",\n    args: [connectedAddress, tipJar?.address],\n    watch: true,\n  });\n\n  const { writeContractAsync: approveUsdc } = useScaffoldWriteContract({ contractName: \"USDC\" });\n  const { writeContractAsync: sendTip, isMining } = useScaffoldWriteContract({ contractName: \"TipJar\" });\n\n  const [isApproving, setIsApproving] = useState(false);\n  const busy = isApproving || isMining;\n\n  const handleTip = async () => {\n    if (!connectedAddress || !tipJar) {\n      notification.error(\"Connect a wallet first.\");\n      return;\n    }\n\n    let parsedAmount: bigint;\n    try {\n      parsedAmount = parseUnits(amount || \"0\", USDC_DECIMALS);\n    } catch {\n      notification.error(\"Enter a valid USDC amount.\");\n      return;\n    }\n    if (parsedAmount === 0n) {\n      notification.error(\"Tip amount must be greater than zero.\");\n      return;\n    }\n    if (balance !== undefined && parsedAmount > balance) {\n      notification.error(\"Not enough USDC in your wallet.\");\n      return;\n    }\n\n    try {\n      // USDC is pulled with transferFrom, so approve the jar for at least this tip first.\n      if ((allowance ?? 0n) < parsedAmount) {\n        setIsApproving(true);\n        await approveUsdc({ functionName: \"approve\", args: [tipJar.address, parsedAmount] });\n        setIsApproving(false);\n      }\n\n      await sendTip({ functionName: \"tip\", args: [parsedAmount, message] });\n      setAmount(\"\");\n      setMessage(\"\");\n    } catch (error) {\n      console.error(\"Tip failed\", error);\n    } finally {\n      setIsApproving(false);\n    }\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"card bg-base-100 shadow-xl\">\n      <div className=\"card-body gap-4\">\n        <h2 className=\"card-title\">Send a tip</h2>\n\n        <label className=\"form-control\">\n          <span className=\"label-text mb-1\">Amount (USDC)</span>\n          <input\n            type=\"number\"\n            min=\"0\"\n            step=\"0.01\"\n            placeholder=\"5.00\"\n            className=\"input input-bordered\"\n            value={amount}\n            onChange={e => setAmount(e.target.value)}\n          />\n        </label>\n\n        <label className=\"form-control\">\n          <span className=\"label-text mb-1\">Message</span>\n          <textarea\n            placeholder=\"Say something nice…\"\n            className=\"textarea textarea-bordered\"\n            value={message}\n            onChange={e => setMessage(e.target.value)}\n          />\n        </label>\n\n        {connectedAddress && balance !== undefined && (\n          <p className=\"text-sm text-base-content/60\">\n            Wallet balance: {Number(formatUnits(balance, USDC_DECIMALS)).toLocaleString()} USDC\n          </p>\n        )}\n\n        <button className=\"btn btn-primary\" onClick={handleTip} disabled={busy || !connectedAddress}>\n          {busy && <span className=\"loading loading-spinner loading-sm\" />}\n          {isApproving ? \"Approving USDC…\" : isMining ? \"Sending tip…\" : \"Tip USDC\"}\n        </button>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1121,"cache_read_input_tokens":65937,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1121},"output_tokens":4,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"629310d9-136f-4f61-a7ad-95f98c72edf2","uuid":"b2e21ae2-a411-4cda-a1b6-933b0e27e862","timestamp":"2026-08-19T18:52:08.292Z","request_id":"req_011CeCcQUkmNwKDRqPi2ijLN"}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01T3YnX8gXSSv3F1nsEZMpZY","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t# 🏗 Scaffold-ETH 2\n2\t\n3\t<h4 align=\"center\">\n4\t  <a href=\"https://docs.scaffoldeth.io\">Documentation</a> |\n5\t  <a href=\"https://scaffoldeth.io\">Website</a>\n6\t</h4>\n7\t\n8\t🧪 An open-source, up-to-date toolkit for building decentralized applications (dapps) on the Ethereum blockchain. It's designed to make it easier for developers to create and deploy smart contracts and build user interfaces that interact with those contracts.\n9\t\n10\t> [!NOTE]\n11\t> 🤖 Scaffold-ETH 2 is AI-ready! It has everything agents need to build on Ethereum. Check `.agents/`, `.claude/`, `.opencode` or `.cursor/` for more info.\n12\t\n13\t⚙️ Built using NextJS, RainbowKit, Foundry, Wagmi, Viem, and Typescript.\n14\t\n15\t- ✅ **Contract Hot Reload**: Your frontend auto-adapts to your smart contract as you edit it.\n16\t- 🪝 **[Custom hooks](https://docs.scaffoldeth.io/hooks/)**: Collection of React hooks wrapper around [wagmi](https://wagmi.sh/) to simplify interactions with smart contracts with typescript autocompletion.\n17\t- 🧱 [**Components**](https://docs.scaffoldeth.io/components/): Collection of common web3 components to quickly build your frontend.\n18\t- 🔥 **Burner Wallet & Local Faucet**: Quickly test your application with a burner wallet and local faucet.\n19\t- 🔐 **Integration with Wallet Providers**: Connect to different wallet providers and interact with the Ethereum network.\n20\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"629310d9-136f-4f61-a7ad-95f98c72edf2","uuid":"80fb7c92-0c98-433d-b526-509acb5196e6","timestamp":"2026-08-19T18:55:40.093Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/ssd/workspace/buidlguidl/ethskills-evals-test/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-001/2026-08-19T184304Z-claude-with-skill-2/workspace/README.md","content":"# 🏗 Scaffold-ETH 2\n\n<h4 align=\"center\">\n  <a href=\"https://docs.scaffoldeth.io\">Documentation</a> |\n  <a href=\"https://scaffoldeth.io\">Website</a>\n</h4>\n\n🧪 An open-source, up-to-date toolkit for building decentralized applications (dapps) on the Ethereum blockchain. It's designed to make it easier for developers to create and deploy smart contracts and build user interfaces that interact with those contracts.\n\n> [!NOTE]\n> 🤖 Scaffold-ETH 2 is AI-ready! It has everything agents need to build on Ethereum. Check `.agents/`, `.claude/`, `.opencode` or `.cursor/` for more info.\n\n⚙️ Built using NextJS, RainbowKit, Foundry, Wagmi, Viem, and Typescript.\n\n- ✅ **Contract Hot Reload**: Your frontend auto-adapts to your smart contract as you edit it.\n- 🪝 **[Custom hooks](https://docs.scaffoldeth.io/hooks/)**: Collection of React hooks wrapper around [wagmi](https://wagmi.sh/) to simplify interactions with smart contracts with typescript autocompletion.\n- 🧱 [**Components**](https://docs.scaffoldeth.io/components/): Collection of common web3 components to quickly build your frontend.\n- 🔥 **Burner Wallet & Local Faucet**: Quickly test your application with a burner wallet and local faucet.\n- 🔐 **Integration with Wallet Providers**: Connect to different wallet providers and interact with the Ethereum network.\n","numLines":20,"startLine":1,"totalLines":83}}}
{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CeCchgaddtVMFbu1Ngff5","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_011GnjiU98N7V56phKX9RatS","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/ssd/workspace/buidlguidl/ethskills-evals-test/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-001/2026-08-19T184304Z-claude-with-skill-2/workspace/README.md","content":"# USDC Tip Jar (Base)\n\nA Scaffold-ETH 2 dApp: a `TipJar` contract that accepts **USDC tips** on Base\nwith a public on-chain message feed, and a page to send a tip and watch the feed\nupdate live.\n\n- **Contract:** `packages/foundry/contracts/TipJar.sol` — pulls USDC via\n  `transferFrom` (tippers `approve` the jar first), records each tip\n  (`tipper`, `amount`, `timestamp`, `message`), and lets the owner withdraw.\n- **Frontend:** `packages/nextjs/app/page.tsx` — total-tipped stats, a\n  tip form (approve + tip), and the newest-first feed.\n- **USDC:** Circle native USDC on Base — `0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913`\n  (6 decimals). Registered for the frontend in\n  `packages/nextjs/contracts/externalContracts.ts`.\n\nDeploying to a live network? See **[DEPLOY.md](./DEPLOY.md)** for the static\nIPFS build and release checklist.\n\n## Why we develop against a Base fork\n\nUSDC is a real token with real holders. To build and demo the tip jar against\n**real Base state** — the actual USDC contract, real balances — without risking\nreal money, we run a **local Anvil fork of Base** (chain id `31337`). Nothing is\nbroadcast to Base; the fork is a private local copy. Demo tips move **real USDC**\nbetween local test identities that we fund from an existing on-chain holder.\n\nThe frontend already targets the local fork: `scaffold.config.ts` sets\n`targetNetworks: [chains.foundry]` (chain id `31337`).\n\n## Prerequisites\n\n- [Node](https://nodejs.org/) >= 20.18.3, [Yarn](https://yarnpkg.com/), and\n  [Foundry](https://book.getfoundry.sh/getting-started/installation) (`anvil`, `cast`).\n\n## Local workflow\n\nRun each step in its own terminal from the repo root.\n\n### 1. Install\n\n```bash\nyarn install\n```\n\n### 2. Fork Base\n\n```bash\nyarn fork          # anvil --fork-url base --chain-id 31337 (defaults to Base)\n```\n\nThis forks Base mainnet locally. The USDC contract and all real balances are\nnow available at `http://127.0.0.1:8545`.\n\n### 3. Deploy the TipJar\n\n```bash\nyarn deploy        # deploys TipJar wired to Base USDC, exports ABI to the frontend\n```\n\nThe deploy owner is the default local account\n`0xa0Ee7A142d267C1f36714E4a8F75612F20a79720` (Anvil's `scaffold-eth-default`).\n\n### 4. Start the frontend\n\n```bash\nyarn start         # http://localhost:3000\n```\n\nThe page connects to the local fork with a burner wallet (shown in the top-right).\n\n### 5. Fund a test identity with real USDC\n\nUSDC only moves if a test wallet holds some. Grab an existing USDC holder on the\nfork and impersonate it to fund your wallet. Below, `WALLET` is the address you\nwant to tip from — **copy the burner address from the app**, or use an Anvil\naccount for the CLI demo.\n\n```bash\nexport RPC=http://127.0.0.1:8545\nexport USDC=0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913\nexport WHALE=0xcDAC0d6c6C59727a65F871236188350531885C43   # a current USDC holder on Base\nexport WALLET=<your burner address from the app>\n\n# Impersonated accounts need ETH for gas, then send USDC to your wallet:\ncast rpc anvil_setBalance $WHALE 0xde0b6b3a7640000 --rpc-url $RPC        # 1 ETH\ncast rpc anvil_impersonateAccount $WHALE --rpc-url $RPC\ncast send $USDC \"transfer(address,uint256)\" $WALLET 100000000 \\\n  --from $WHALE --unlocked --rpc-url $RPC                                # 100 USDC (6 decimals)\ncast rpc anvil_stopImpersonatingAccount $WHALE --rpc-url $RPC\n\ncast call $USDC \"balanceOf(address)(uint256)\" $WALLET --rpc-url $RPC     # confirm 100000000\n```\n\n> The whale address above holds millions of USDC on Base today, but any current\n> holder works. If you'd rather not depend on a specific holder, grant USDC\n> deterministically by writing the balance mapping directly (slot `9` on USDC):\n>\n> ```bash\n> SLOT=$(cast index address $WALLET 9)\n> cast rpc anvil_setStorageAt $USDC $SLOT \\\n>   0x0000000000000000000000000000000000000000000000000000000005f5e100 --rpc-url $RPC  # 100 USDC\n> ```\n\n### 6. Tip\n\nIn the app: enter an amount and message and click **Tip USDC**. The first tip\nprompts an `approve` (USDC is pulled with `transferFrom`), then the tip itself.\nThe feed and totals update live. Repeat with a second burner/identity to see\nmultiple tippers.\n\nPrefer the CLI? This moves real USDC end to end (Anvil account #0 as the tipper):\n\n```bash\nJAR=$(cast call ... )   # or read TipJar's address from packages/nextjs/contracts/deployedContracts.ts\nPK=0xac0974bec39a17e36ba4a6b4d238ff944bacb478cbed5efcae784d7bf4f2ff80  # Anvil #0\ncast send $USDC \"approve(address,uint256)\" $JAR 25000000 --private-key $PK --rpc-url $RPC\ncast send $JAR  \"tip(uint256,string)\" 25000000 \"Loving the tip jar!\" --private-key $PK --rpc-url $RPC\ncast call $JAR  \"recentTips(uint256)((address,uint256,uint256,string)[])\" 5 --rpc-url $RPC\n```\n\n### Live timestamps (optional)\n\nAnvil only mines when a transaction arrives, so `block.timestamp` freezes between\ntips. For a demo where the feed timestamps should track wall-clock time, enable\ninterval mining (or add `--block-time 1` to the fork):\n\n```bash\ncast rpc anvil_setIntervalMining 1 --rpc-url $RPC\n```\n\n## Tests\n\n```bash\nyarn foundry:test\n```\n\nUnit tests use a 6-decimal mock USDC to cover tip/feed/withdraw logic; the real\nUSDC is exercised through the fork workflow above.\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1557,"cache_read_input_tokens":82872,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1557},"output_tokens":20,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"629310d9-136f-4f61-a7ad-95f98c72edf2","uuid":"7d8a07d2-5ef9-4b13-bcb1-09a67512d257","timestamp":"2026-08-19T18:56:14.933Z","request_id":"req_011CeCchfYNkTqkxxV19ddfo"}

Registered for the frontend in\n  `packages/nextjs/contracts/externalContracts.ts`.\n\nDeploying to a live network? See **[DEPLOY.md](./DEPLOY.md)** for the static\nIPFS build and release checklist.\n\n## Why we develop against a Base fork\n\nUSDC is a real token with real holders. To build and demo the tip jar against\n**real Base state** — the actual USDC contract, real balances — without risking\nreal money, we run a **local Anvil fork of Base** (chain id `31337`). Nothing is\nbroadcast to Base; the fork is a private local copy. Demo tips move **real USDC**\nbetween local test identities that we fund from an existing on-chain holder.\n\nThe frontend already targets the local fork: `scaffold.config.ts` sets\n`targetNetworks: [chains.foundry]` (chain id `31337`).\n\n## Prerequisites\n\n- [Node](https://nodejs.org/) >= 20.18.3, [Yarn](https://yarnpkg.com/), and\n  [Foundry](https://book.getfoundry.sh/getting-started/installation) (`anvil`, `cast`).\n\n## Local workflow\n\nRun each step in its own terminal from the repo root.\n\n### 1. Install\n\n```bash\nyarn install\n```\n\n### 2. Fork Base\n\n```bash\nyarn fork          # anvil --fork-url base --chain-id 31337 (defaults to Base)\n```\n\nThis forks Base mainnet locally. The USDC contract and all real balances are\nnow available at `http://127.0.0.1:8545`.\n\n### 3. Start the frontend\n\n```bash\nyarn start         # http://localhost:3000\n```\n\nThe page connects to the local fork with a burner wallet (shown in the top-right).\n\n### 5. Fund a test identity with real USDC\n\nUSDC only moves if a test wallet holds some. Grab an existing USDC holder on the\nfork and impersonate it to fund your wallet. Below, `WALLET` is the address you\nwant to tip from — **copy the burner address from the app**, or use an Anvil\naccount for the CLI demo.\n\n```bash\nexport RPC=http://127.0.0.1:8545\nexport USDC=0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913\nexport WHALE=0xcDAC0d6c6C59727a65F871236188350531885C43   # a current USDC holder on Base\nexport WALLET=<your burner address from the app>\n\n# Impersonated accounts need ETH for gas, then send USDC to your wallet:\ncast rpc anvil_setBalance $WHALE 0xde0b6b3a7640000 --rpc-url $RPC        # 1 ETH\ncast rpc anvil_impersonateAccount $WHALE --rpc-url $RPC\ncast send $USDC \"transfer(address,uint256)\" $WALLET 100000000 \\\n  --from $WHALE --unlocked --rpc-url $RPC                                # 100 USDC (6 decimals)\ncast rpc anvil_stopImpersonatingAccount $WHALE --rpc-url $RPC\n\ncast call $USDC \"balanceOf(address)(uint256)\" $WALLET --rpc-url $RPC     # confirm 100000000\n```\n\n> The whale address above holds millions of USDC on Base today, but any current\n> holder works. If you'd rather not depend on a specific holder, grant USDC\n> deterministically by writing the balance mapping directly (slot `9` on USDC):\n>\n> ```bash\n> SLOT=$(cast index address $WALLET 9)\n> cast rpc anvil_setStorageAt $USDC $SLOT \\\n>   0x0000000000000000000000000000000000000000000000000000000005f5e100 --rpc-url $RPC  # 100 USDC\n> ```\n\n### 6. Tip\n\nIn the app: enter an amount and message and click **Tip USDC**. The first tip\nprompts an `approve` (USDC is pulled with `transferFrom`), then the tip itself.\nThe feed and totals update live. Repeat with a second burner/identity to see\nmultiple tippers.\n\nPrefer the CLI? This moves real USDC end to end (Anvil account #0 as the tipper):\n\n```bash\nJAR=$(cast call ... )   # or read TipJar's address from packages/nextjs/contracts/deployedContracts.ts\nPK=0xac0974bec39a17e36ba4a6b4d238ff944bacb478cbed5efcae784d7bf4f2ff80  # Anvil #0\ncast send $USDC \"approve(address,uint256)\" $JAR 25000000 --private-key $PK --rpc-url $RPC\ncast send $JAR  \"tip(uint256,string)\" 25000000 \"Loving the tip jar!\" --private-key $PK --rpc-url $RPC\ncast call $JAR  \"recentTips(uint256)((address,uint256,uint256,string)[])\" 5 --rpc-url $RPC\n```\n\n### Live timestamps (optional)\n\nAnvil only mines when a transaction arrives, so `block.timestamp` freezes between\ntips. For a demo where the feed timestamps should track wall-clock time, enable\ninterval mining (or add `--block-time 1` to the fork):\n\n```bash\ncast rpc anvil_setIntervalMining 1 --rpc-url $RPC\n```\n\n## Tests\n\n```bash\nyarn foundry:test\n```\n\nUnit tests use a 6-decimal mock USDC to cover tip/feed/withdraw logic; the real\nUSDC is exercised through the fork workflow above.\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":1,"oldLines":83,"newStart":1,"newLines":138,"lines":["-# 🏗 Scaffold-ETH 2","+# USDC Tip Jar (Base)"," ","-<h4 align=\"center\">","-  <a href=\"https://docs.scaffoldeth.io\">Documentation</a> |","-  <a href=\"https://scaffoldeth.io\">Website</a>","-</h4>","+A Scaffold-ETH 2 dApp: a `TipJar` contract that accepts **USDC tips** on Base","+with a public on-chain message feed, and a page to send a tip and watch the feed","+update live."," ","-🧪 An open-source, up-to-date toolkit for building decentralized applications (dapps) on the Ethereum blockchain. It's designed to make it easier for developers to create and deploy smart contracts and build user interfaces that interact with those contracts.","+- **Contract:** `packages/foundry/contracts/TipJar.sol` — pulls USDC via","+  `transferFrom` (tippers `approve` the jar first), records each tip","+  (`tipper`, `amount`, `timestamp`, `message`), and lets the owner withdraw.","+- **Frontend:** `packages/nextjs/app/page.tsx` — total-tipped stats, a","+  tip form (approve + tip), and the newest-first feed.","+- **USDC:** Circle native USDC on Base — `0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913`","+  (6 decimals). Registered for the frontend in","+  `packages/nextjs/contracts/externalContracts.ts`."," ","-> [!NOTE]","-> 🤖 Scaffold-ETH 2 is AI-ready! It has everything agents need to build on Ethereum. Check `.agents/`, `.claude/`, `.opencode` or `.cursor/` for more info.","+Deploying to a live network? See **[DEPLOY.md](./DEPLOY.md)** for the static","+IPFS build and release checklist."," ","-⚙️ Built using NextJS, RainbowKit, Foundry, Wagmi, Viem, and Typescript.","+## Why we develop against a Base fork"," ","-- ✅ **Contract Hot Reload**: Your frontend auto-adapts to your smart contract as you edit it.","-- 🪝 **[Custom hooks](https://docs.scaffoldeth.io/hooks/)**: Collection of React hooks wrapper around [wagmi](https://wagmi.sh/) to simplify interactions with smart contracts with typescript autocompletion.","-- 🧱 [**Components**](https://docs.scaffoldeth.io/components/): Collection of common web3 components to quickly build your frontend.","-- 🔥 **Burner Wallet & Local Faucet**: Quickly test your application with a burner wallet and local faucet.","-- 🔐 **Integration with Wallet Providers**: Connect to different wallet providers and interact with the Ethereum network.","+USDC is a real token with real holders. To build and demo the tip jar against","+**real Base state** — the actual USDC contract, real balances — without risking","+real money, we run a **local Anvil fork of Base** (chain id `31337`). Nothing is","+broadcast to Base; the fork is a private local copy. Demo tips move **real USDC**","+between local test identities that we fund from an existing on-chain holder."," ","-![Debug Contracts tab](https://github.com/scaffold-eth/scaffold-eth-2/assets/55535804/b237af0c-5027-4849-a5c1-2e31495cccb1)","+The frontend already targets the local fork: `scaffold.config.ts` sets","+`targetNetworks: [chains.foundry]` (chain id `31337`)."," ","-## Requirements","+## Prerequisites"," ","-Before you begin, you need to install the following tools:","+- [Node](https://nodejs.org/) >= 20.18.3, [Yarn](https://yarnpkg.com/), and","+  [Foundry](https://book.getfoundry.sh/getting-started/installation) (`anvil`, `cast`)."," ","-- [Node (>= v20.18.3)](https://nodejs.org/en/download/)","-- Yarn ([v1](https://classic.yarnpkg.com/en/docs/install/) or [v2+](https://yarnpkg.com/getting-started/install))","-- [Git](https://git-scm.com/downloads)","+## Local workflow"," ","-## Quickstart","+Run each step in its own terminal from the repo root."," ","-To get started with Scaffold-ETH 2, follow the steps below:","+### 1. This moves real USDC end to end (Anvil account #0 as the tipper):"," ","-## Documentation","+```bash","+JAR=$(cast call ... )   # or read TipJar's address from packages/nextjs/contracts/deployedContracts.ts","+PK=0xac0974bec39a17e36ba4a6b4d238ff944bacb478cbed5efcae784d7bf4f2ff80  # Anvil #0","+cast send $USDC \"approve(address,uint256)\" $JAR 25000000 --private-key $PK --rpc-url $RPC","+cast send $JAR  \"tip(uint256,string)\" 25000000 \"Loving the tip jar!\" --private-key $PK --rpc-url $RPC","+cast call $JAR  \"recentTips(uint256)((address,uint256,uint256,string)[])\" 5 --rpc-url $RPC","+```"," ","-Visit our [docs](https://docs.scaffoldeth.io) to learn how to start building with Scaffold-ETH 2.","+### Live timestamps (optional)"," ","-To know more about its features, check out our [website](https://scaffoldeth.io).","+Anvil only mines when a transaction arrives, so `block.timestamp` freezes between","+tips. For a demo where the feed timestamps should track wall-clock time, enable","+interval mining (or add `--block-time 1` to the fork):"," ","-## Contributing to Scaffold-ETH 2","+```bash","+cast rpc anvil_setIntervalMining 1 --rpc-url $RPC","+```"," ","-We welcome contributions to Scaffold-ETH 2!","+## Tests"," ","-Please see [CONTRIBUTING.MD](https://github.com/scaffold-eth/scaffold-eth-2/blob/main/CONTRIBUTING.md) for more information and guidelines for contributing to Scaffold-ETH 2.","\\ No newline at end of file","+```bash","+yarn foundry:test","+```","+","+Unit tests use a 6-decimal mock USDC to cover tip/feed/withdraw logic; the real","+USDC is exercised through the fork workflow above."]}],"originalFile":"# 🏗 Scaffold-ETH 2\n\n<h4 align=\"center\">\n  <a href=\"https://docs.scaffoldeth.io\">Documentation</a> |\n  <a href=\"https://scaffoldeth.io\">Website</a>\n</h4>\n\n🧪 An open-source, up-to-date toolkit for building decentralized applications (dapps) on the Ethereum blockchain. It's designed to make it easier for developers to create and deploy smart contracts and build user interfaces that interact with those contracts.\n\n> [!NOTE]\n> 🤖 Scaffold-ETH 2 is AI-ready! It has everything agents need to build on Ethereum. Check `.agents/`, `.claude/`, `.opencode` or `.cursor/` for more info.\n\n⚙️ Built using NextJS, RainbowKit, Foundry, Wagmi, Viem, and Typescript.\n\n- ✅ **Contract Hot Reload**: Your frontend auto-adapts to your smart contract as you edit it.\n- 🪝 **[Custom hooks](https://docs.scaffoldeth.io/hooks/)**: Collection of React hooks wrapper around [wagmi](https://wagmi.sh/) to simplify interactions with smart contracts with typescript autocompletion.\n- 🧱 [**Components**](https://docs.scaffoldeth.io/components/): Collection of common web3 components to quickly build your frontend.\n- 🔥 **Burner Wallet & Local Faucet**: Quickly test your application with a burner wallet and local faucet.\n- 🔐 **Integration with Wallet Providers**: Connect to different wallet providers and interact with the Ethereum network.\n\n![Debug Contracts tab](https://github.com/scaffold-eth/scaffold-eth-2/assets/55535804/b237af0c-5027-4849-a5c1-2e31495cccb1)\n\n## Requirements\n\nBefore you begin, you need to install the following tools:\n\n- [Node (>= v20.18.3)](https://nodejs.org/en/download/)\n- Yarn ([v1](https://classic.yarnpkg.com/en/docs/install/) or [v2+](https://yarnpkg.com/getting-started/install))\n- [Git](https://git-scm.com/downloads)\n\n## Quickstart\n\nTo get started with Scaffold-ETH 2, follow the steps below:\n\n1. Install dependencies if it was skipped in CLI:\n\n```\ncd my-dapp-example\nyarn install\n```\n\n2. Run a local network in the first terminal:\n\n```\nyarn chain\n```\n\nThis command starts a local Ethereum network using Foundry. The network runs on your local machine and can be used for testing and development. You can customize the network configuration in `packages/foundry/foundry.toml`.\n\n3. On a second terminal, deploy the test contract:\n\n```\nyarn deploy\n```\n\nThis command deploys a test smart contract to the local network. The contract is located in `packages/foundry/contracts` and can be modified to suit your needs. The `yarn deploy` command uses the deploy script located in `packages/foundry/script` to deploy the contract to the network. You can also customize the deploy script.\n\n4. On a third terminal, start your NextJS app:\n\n```\nyarn start\n```\n\nVisit your app on: `http://localhost:3000`. You can interact with your smart contract using the `Debug Contracts` page. You can tweak the app config in `packages/nextjs/scaffold.config.ts`.\n\nRun smart contract test with `yarn foundry:test`\n\n- Edit your smart contracts in `packages/foundry/contracts`\n- Edit your frontend homepage at `packages/nextjs/app/page.tsx`. For guidance on [routing](https://nextjs.org/docs/app/building-your-application/routing/defining-routes) and configuring [pages/layouts](https://nextjs.org/docs/app/building-your-application/routing/pages-and-layouts) checkout the Next.js documentation.\n- Edit your deployment scripts in `packages/foundry/script`\n\n\n## Documentation\n\nVisit our [docs](https://docs.scaffoldeth.io) to learn how to start building with Scaffold-ETH 2.\n\nTo know more about its features, check out our [website](https://scaffoldeth.io).\n\n## Contributing to Scaffold-ETH 2\n\nWe welcome contributions to Scaffold-ETH 2!\n\nPlease see [CONTRIBUTING.MD](https://github.com/scaffold-eth/scaffold-eth-2/blob/main/CONTRIBUTING.md) for more information and guidelines for contributing to Scaffold-ETH 2.","userModified":false}}

:4,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"629310d9-136f-4f61-a7ad-95f98c72edf2","uuid":"b112e1bd-1238-4b3a-b38d-bbf64c5297a6","timestamp":"2026-08-19T18:56:22.443Z","request_id":"req_011CeCckEkhkZGPwh5pbsDN8"}
{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CeCckG36PMKFGNjPau75B","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Now DEPLOY.md with the build, upload, and verification commands:"}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2532,"cache_read_input_tokens":84429,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":2532},"output_tokens":4,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"629310d9-136f-4f61-a7ad-95f98c72edf2","uuid":"82744f28-df41-4402-b80b-a4451c70e610","timestamp":"2026-08-19T18:56:22.444Z","request_id":"req_011CeCckEkhkZGPwh5pbsDN8"}
{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CeCckG36PMKFGNjPau75B","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01Apkna8SGVUMC5Pq3q2N92f","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/ssd/workspace/buidlguidl/ethskills-evals-test/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-001/2026-08-19T184304Z-claude-with-skill-2/workspace/DEPLOY.md","content":"# Deploying the USDC Tip Jar to IPFS\n\nThe site ships as a **static export** hosted on IPFS. This document lists the\nexact build, upload, and post-deploy verification commands. It does **not** run\nan upload — running these commands is a deliberate release step.\n\n## 0. Point the frontend at the network you are releasing for\n\nThe static bundle bakes in its target network at build time.\n\n- **Public release on Base mainnet:** deploy `TipJar` to Base\n  (`yarn deploy --network base`, which needs a funded keystore account) and set\n  the frontend target to Base in `packages/nextjs/scaffold.config.ts`:\n\n  ```ts\n  import * as chains from \"viem/chains\";\n  // ...\n  targetNetworks: [chains.base],\n  ```\n\n  USDC is already registered for Base (chain id `8453`) in\n  `packages/nextjs/contracts/externalContracts.ts`, and the `TipJar` address on\n  `8453` must be present in `deployedContracts.ts` after the Base deploy.\n\n- **Shipping a fork-backed demo build** (targets `chains.foundry`, chain id\n  `31337`): leave `scaffold.config.ts` as-is. The site then only works for\n  visitors running a local Base fork (see [README.md](./README.md)).\n\nThe build config itself lives in `packages/nextjs/next.config.ts` and is gated\non `NEXT_PUBLIC_IPFS_BUILD`:\n\n```ts\nif (process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_IPFS_BUILD === \"true\") {\n  nextConfig.output = \"export\";     // static HTML export -> out/\n  nextConfig.trailingSlash = true;  // /route/ -> out/route/index.html\n  nextConfig.images = { unoptimized: true };\n}\n```\n\n## 1. Build the static export\n\nSet the production origin so Open Graph URLs/images resolve to the deployed\ndomain, not localhost. Remove stale artifacts first.\n\n```bash\ncd packages/nextjs\nrm -rf .next out\n\nNEXT_PUBLIC_PRODUCTION_URL=\"https://<your-production-domain>\" \\\n  NEXT_PUBLIC_IPFS_BUILD=true \\\n  yarn build\n```\n\n> **Node 25 note:** on Node 25 the built-in `localStorage` global can lack the\n> standard Web Storage methods, which crashes wallet libraries during static\n> prerender. If you hit that, run the build with a process-level remedy so the\n> build workers inherit it:\n>\n> ```bash\n> NODE_OPTIONS=\"--localstorage-file=.node-localstorage\" \\\n>   NEXT_PUBLIC_PRODUCTION_URL=\"https://<your-production-domain>\" \\\n>   NEXT_PUBLIC_IPFS_BUILD=true \\\n>   yarn build\n> ```\n>\n> (This project builds cleanly on Node 22 without it.)\n\n## 2. Verify the export before uploading\n\n```bash\ncd packages/nextjs\n\n# One directory with an index.html per route (/, /debug, /blockexplorer, ...):\nls out/*/index.html\n\n# Open Graph tags carry the production origin, not localhost:\ngrep -o 'og:url\" content=\"[^\"]*\"'   out/index.html\ngrep -o 'og:image\" content=\"[^\"]*\"' out/index.html\n\n# Sanity-check routes resolve when served as static files:\npython3 -m http.server 8899 --directory out &\ncurl -s -o /dev/null -w \"root:  %{http_code}\\n\"  http://127.0.0.1:8899/\ncurl -s -o /dev/null -w \"debug: %{http_code}\\n\" -L http://127.0.0.1:8899/debug/\nkill %1\n```\n\nEvery route must have its own `index.html`, and the OG origin must be your\nproduction domain.\n\n## 3. Upload to IPFS\n\nUsing the bundled bgipfs uploader (it builds with `NEXT_PUBLIC_IPFS_BUILD=true`,\nso pass the production origin through the environment to keep OG tags correct):\n\n```bash\ncd packages/nextjs\nNEXT_PUBLIC_PRODUCTION_URL=\"https://<your-production-domain>\" yarn ipfs\n```\n\nOr upload the `out/` directory you built in step 1 with your own IPFS tooling,\ne.g.:\n\n```bash\nipfs add -r --cid-version 1 packages/nextjs/out\n```\n\nRecord the resulting **CID**.\n\n## 4. Verify after uploading\n\n1. **Record the CID.** If it is unchanged from a previous upload, the bytes are\n   identical — recheck the build output and upload target before blaming\n   gateway caching.\n\n2. **Load the root and at least one non-home route** through a public gateway.\n   Root success alone does not prove exported routes resolve.\n\n   ```bash\n   curl -s -o /dev/null -w \"root:  %{http_code}\\n\" -L \"https://<gateway>/ipfs/<cid>/\"\n   curl -s -o /dev/null -w \"debug: %{http_code}\\n\" -L \"https://<gateway>/ipfs/<cid>/debug/\"   # expect 200, not 404\n   ```\n\n3. **Confirm the Open Graph image and URL** use the production origin (view the\n   page source over the gateway and check `og:url` / `og:image`).\n\n4. **ENS (optional):** only after reviewing and approving the deployed CID,\n   update the ENS name's content hash to `ipfs://<cid>`, then confirm the\n   resolver and a public gateway serve that CID:\n\n   ```bash\n   cast resolve-name <name>.eth            # sanity check the name resolves\n   # set the contenthash via the ENS app or your resolver tooling, then:\n   curl -s -o /dev/null -w \"%{http_code}\\n\" -L \"https://<name>.eth.limo/\"\n   ```\n\nDo not announce the release until the root and a non-home route both return\n`200` through the gateway and the OG origin is correct.\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2532,"cache_read_input_tokens":84429,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":2532},"output_tokens":4,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"629310d9-136f-4f61-a7ad-95f98c72edf2","uuid":"2dfd75c1-4092-4c5c-9505-d64518ab8a95","timestamp":"2026-08-19T18:56:47.697Z","request_id":"req_011CeCckEkhkZGPwh5pbsDN8"}

This document lists the\nexact build, upload, and post-deploy verification commands. It does **not** run\nan upload — running these commands is a deliberate release step.\n\n## 0. Point the frontend at the network you are releasing for\n\nThe static bundle bakes in its target network at build time.\n\n- **Public release on Base mainnet:** deploy `TipJar` to Base\n  (`yarn deploy --network base`, which needs a funded keystore account) and set\n  the frontend target to Base in `packages/nextjs/scaffold.config.ts`:\n\n  ```ts\n  import * as chains from \"viem/chains\";\n  // ...\n  targetNetworks: [chains.base],\n  ```\n\n  USDC is already registered for Base (chain id `8453`) in\n  `packages/nextjs/contracts/externalContracts.ts`, and the `TipJar` address on\n  `8453` must be present in `deployedContracts.ts` after the Base deploy.\n\n- **Shipping a fork-backed demo build** (targets `chains.foundry`, chain id\n  `31337`): leave `scaffold.config.ts` as-is. The site then only works for\n  visitors running a local Base fork (see [README.md](./README.md)).\n\nThe build config itself lives in `packages/nextjs/next.config.ts` and is gated\non `NEXT_PUBLIC_IPFS_BUILD`:\n\n```ts\nif (process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_IPFS_BUILD === \"true\") {\n  nextConfig.output = \"export\";     // static HTML export -> out/\n  nextConfig.trailingSlash = true;  // /route/ -> out/route/index.html\n  nextConfig.images = { unoptimized: true };\n}\n```\n\n## 1. Build the static export\n\nSet the production origin so Open Graph URLs/images resolve to the deployed\ndomain, not localhost. Remove stale artifacts first.\n\n```bash\ncd packages/nextjs\nrm -rf .next out\n\nNEXT_PUBLIC_PRODUCTION_URL=\"https://<your-production-domain>\" \\\n  NEXT_PUBLIC_IPFS_BUILD=true \\\n  yarn build\n```\n\n> **Node 25 note:** on Node 25 the built-in `localStorage` global can lack the\n> standard Web Storage methods, which crashes wallet libraries during static\n> prerender. If you hit that, run the build with a process-level remedy so the\n> build workers inherit it:\n>\n> ```bash\n> NODE_OPTIONS=\"--localstorage-file=.node-localstorage\" \\\n>   NEXT_PUBLIC_PRODUCTION_URL=\"https://<your-production-domain>\" \\\n>   NEXT_PUBLIC_IPFS_BUILD=true \\\n>   yarn build\n> ```\n>\n> (This project builds cleanly on Node 22 without it.)\n\n## 2. Verify the export before uploading\n\n```bash\ncd packages/nextjs\n\n# One directory with an index.html per route (/, /debug, /blockexplorer, ...):\nls out/*/index.html\n\n# Open Graph tags carry the production origin, not localhost:\ngrep -o 'og:url\" content=\"[^\"]*\"'   out/index.html\ngrep -o 'og:image\" content=\"[^\"]*\"' out/index.html\n\n# Sanity-check routes resolve when served as static files:\npython3 -m http.server 8899 --directory out &\ncurl -s -o /dev/null -w \"root:  %{http_code}\\n\"  http://127.0.0.1:8899/\ncurl -s -o /dev/null -w \"debug: %{http_code}\\n\" -L http://127.0.0.1:8899/debug/\nkill %1\n```\n\nEvery route must have its own `index.html`, and the OG origin must be your\nproduction domain.\n\n## 3. Upload to IPFS\n\nUsing the bundled bgipfs uploader (it builds with `NEXT_PUBLIC_IPFS_BUILD=true`,\nso pass the production origin through the environment to keep OG tags correct):\n\n```bash\ncd packages/nextjs\nNEXT_PUBLIC_PRODUCTION_URL=\"https://<your-production-domain>\" yarn ipfs\n```\n\nOr upload the `out/` directory you built in step 1 with your own IPFS tooling,\ne.g.:\n\n```bash\nipfs add -r --cid-version 1 packages/nextjs/out\n```\n\nRecord the resulting **CID**.\n\n## 4. Verify after uploading\n\n1. **Record the CID.** If it is unchanged from a previous upload, the bytes are\n   identical — recheck the build output and upload target before blaming\n   gateway caching.\n\n2. **Load the root and at least one non-home route** through a public gateway.\n   Root success alone does not prove exported routes resolve.\n\n   ```bash\n   curl -s -o /dev/null -w \"root:  %{http_code}\\n\" -L \"https://<gateway>/ipfs/<cid>/\"\n   curl -s -o /dev/null -w \"debug: %{http_code}\\n\" -L \"https://<gateway>/ipfs/<cid>/debug/\"   # expect 200, not 404\n   ```\n\n3. **Confirm the Open Graph image and URL** use the production origin (view the\n   page source over the gateway and check `og:url` / `og:image`).\n\n4. **ENS (optional):** only after reviewing and approving the deployed CID,\n   update the ENS name's content hash to `ipfs://<cid>`, then confirm the\n   resolver and a public gateway serve that CID:\n\n   ```bash\n   cast resolve-name <name>.eth            # sanity check the name resolves\n   # set the contenthash via the ENS app or your resolver tooling, then:\n   curl -s -o /dev/null -w \"%{http_code}\\n\" -L \"https://<name>.eth.limo/\"\n   ```\n\nDo not announce the release until the root and a non-home route both return\n`200` through the gateway and the OG origin is correct.\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CeCcrMj53zvVTz2gxwQfA","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Everything is built and verified. The workspace lives under an untracked artifacts directory, so there's nothing to commit (and you didn't ask me to). Here's what shipped.\n\n## What I built\n\n**Contract** — `packages/foundry/contracts/TipJar.sol`\n- Accepts USDC tips via `transferFrom` (tippers `approve` the jar first), records each tip (`tipper`, `amount`, `timestamp`, `message`), tracks `totalTipped`/`tipCount`, and lets the owner `withdraw`. Uses OZ `SafeERC20` + `Ownable`.\n- The feed is served by a `recentTips(limit)` **view** (newest-first) rather than an event scan — deliberate, because event-log queries are unreliable on a fork where the start block is far from genesis.\n- USDC address is a constructor arg; `DeployTipJar.s.sol` wires it to Base USDC `0x8335…2913`, correct on both a Base fork and Base mainnet.\n- 5 unit tests pass (`yarn foundry:test`) using a 6-decimal mock USDC.\n\n**Frontend** — `packages/nextjs/`\n- `app/page.tsx`: total-tipped stats, a tip form (`components/tipjar/TipForm.tsx`, two-step approve→tip), and a live newest-first feed (`TipFeed.tsx`).\n- USDC registered in `externalContracts.ts` for chains 31337 (fork) and 8453 (Base) so scaffold hooks handle approve/allowance/balance.\n- Target network stays `chains.foundry` (31337) so the local fork demo works out of the box.\n\n## Verified end-to-end (not just \"it built\")\n- Forked Base (`yarn fork` → chain 31337, real USDC responds `\"USDC\"`), deployed the jar, **impersonated a real USDC whale to fund two Anvil identities, and sent two real USDC tips** (25 + 10). Jar holds 35 real USDC; `recentTips` returned both, newest-first. (I had to `anvil_setBalance` the whale for gas first — that's captured in the README.)\n- Static IPFS build: `NEXT_PUBLIC_IPFS_BUILD=true` + production URL → per-route `index.html` dirs, all routes 200 when served, and OG `og:url`/`og:image` carry the production origin (not localhost). I had to teach `getMetadata.ts` to honor `NEXT_PUBLIC_PRODUCTION_URL`, since it only read the Vercel var before.\n- Typecheck + lint + `forge fmt` clean.\n\n## Docs\n- **README.md** — the exact local fork workflow: `yarn fork` (defaulted to Base) → deploy → start → fund a test identity from a real holder (with a deterministic `anvil_setStorageAt` slot-9 fallback) → tip, plus the interval-mining note for live timestamps.\n- **DEPLOY.md** — network-target choice, static build commands (with the Node 25 `localStorage` remedy), pre-upload `out/` checks, the upload command (`yarn ipfs`), and post-upload verification (record CID, curl root + a non-home route through the gateway, OG origin, ENS content hash). Per your constraint, **I did not upload anything.**\n\nOne thing worth flagging: for a genuine *public* IPFS release the site must target Base mainnet, which means deploying `TipJar` to Base and flipping `scaffold.config.ts` to `chains.base` — DEPLOY.md step 0 spells this out. As-is, the default build targets the local fork (correct for the \"develop and demo locally\" constraint)."}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":329,"cache_read_input_tokens":93181,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":329},"output_tokens":6,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"629310d9-136f-4f61-a7ad-95f98c72edf2","uuid":"8b774711-6b07-4f1d-9054-09b97e91eb64","timestamp":"2026-08-19T18:58:00.491Z","request_id":"req_011CeCcrLdLWxVfTfSW23dgi"}
